# Spot Expansion Exploration

Generated: `2026-06-03T06:57:26.121060Z`

Scope and method: live AWS API data using profile `lsmc`. Rows are included only when EC2 says the instance type is offered in the AZ, supports Spot, has current/recent Linux/UNIX Spot price history in that AZ, has `ProcessorInfo.Manufacturer=Intel`, has `>=192` vCPU, has `>500 GiB` memory, and the AWS Pricing API Linux shared-tenancy `processorFeatures` contains `Intel AVX512`.

Pricing notes: `Dedicated $/hr` is the regional EC2 Pricing API Linux/NA `tenancy=Dedicated`, `capacitystatus=Used` hourly rate. Spot prices are AZ-specific and time-stamped. Memory prices use GiB from EC2 `MemoryInfo.SizeInMiB`.

Config boundary: this document is inventory only. Existing non-`bcl2fq` partitions remain unchanged. Suitability is scoped to possible `bcl2fq` use only, not an instruction to modify config.

## Strict AVX512 Exclusion Notes

The following offered Intel Spot-capable >=192-vCPU >500-GiB rows were filtered out where AWS Pricing did not advertise `Intel AVX512` for that instance type in the region. This is intentionally strict; it avoids treating “Intel” alone as proof of AVX512.

- `ap-south-1/ap-south-1a`: `p5en.48xlarge`
- `ap-south-1/ap-south-1b`: `p5en.48xlarge`
- `us-east-1/us-east-1b`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p5en.48xlarge`, `p6-b200.48xlarge`
- `us-east-1/us-east-1d`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p5en.48xlarge`
- `us-west-2/us-west-2a`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p5en.48xlarge`, `p6-b200.48xlarge`
- `us-west-2/us-west-2b`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p6-b200.48xlarge`
- `us-west-2/us-west-2c`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p5en.48xlarge`
- `us-west-2/us-west-2d`: `g7e.48xlarge`, `p5en.48xlarge`, `p6-b200.48xlarge`
